We formulate the problem as a hex-grid semi-Markov decision process (semi-MDP) with mixed actions -- discrete actions for serving, repositioning, and charging, together with continuous charging power -- and variable action durations. To guarantee physical feasibility during both training and deployment, the policy learns over high-level intentions produced by a masked, temperature-annealed actor. These intentions are projected at every decision step through a time-limited rolling mixed-integer linear program (MILP) that strictly enforces state-of-charge, port, and feeder constraints. To mitigate distributional shifts, we optimize a Soft Actor--Critic (SAC) agent against a Wasserstein-1 ambiguity set with a graph-aligned Mahalanobis ground metric that captures spatial correlations. The robust backup uses the Kantorovich--Rubinstein dual, a projected subgradient inner loop, and a primal--dual risk-budget update. Our architecture combines a two-layer Graph Convolutional Network (GCN) encoder, twin critics, and a value network that drives the adversary. Experiments on a large-scale EV fleet simulator built from NYC taxi data show that PD--RSAC achieves the highest net profit, reaching \$1.22M, compared with \$0.58M--\$0.70M for strong heuristic, single-agent RL, and multi-agent RL baselines, including Greedy, SAC, MAPPO, and MADDPG, while maintaining zero feeder-limit violations.

</details>


### [111] [ADEMA: A Knowledge-State Orchestration Architecture for Long-Horizon Knowledge Synthesis with LLMAgents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25849)
*Zhou Hanlin,Chan Huah Yong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ADEMA是一个知识状态编排架构，通过显式认知记账、双评估治理、自适应模式切换等机制，解决长程LLM任务中的知识漂移、隐式承诺和中断断裂问题，实验表明检查点/恢复是唯一关键的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程LLM任务失败的主要原因并非单次答案不可达，而是知识状态在多轮对话中漂移、中间承诺保持隐式、中断打断证据链的演化。

Method: 提出ADEMA架构，结合显式认知记账、异构双评估治理、自适应任务模式切换、声誉塑造的资源分配、检查点可恢复持久化、片段级记忆压缩、制品优先组装、最终有效性检查与安全回退。

Result: 在固定60次运行矩阵中，仅移除检查点/恢复机制导致了唯一的无效运行（发生在中断敏感的恢复条件下）；双评估、片段合成和动态治理是支撑性控制机制，影响轨迹纪律、显式制品进展和成本-质量行为。

Conclusion: 本文的贡献是一种知识状态编排架构，其核心设计承诺是：显式认知状态转换、带有证据的制品进展、以及可恢复的连续性。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM tasks often fail not because a single answer is unattainable, but because knowledge states drift across rounds, intermediate commitments remain implicit, and interruption fractures the evolving evidence chain. This paper presents ADEMA as a knowledge-state orchestration architecture for long-horizon knowledge synthesis rather than as a generic multi-agent runtime. The architecture combines explicit epistemic bookkeeping, heterogeneous dual-evaluator governance, adaptive task-mode switching, reputation-shaped resource allocation, checkpoint-resumable persistence, segment-level memory condensation, artifact-first assembly, and final-validity checking with safe fallback. Evidence is drawn entirely from existing materials: a four-scenario showcase package, a fixed 60-run mechanism matrix, targeted micro-ablation and artifact-chain supplements, and a repaired protocol-level benchmark in which code-oriented evaluation is the clearest quality-sensitive mechanism block. Across the fixed matrix, removing checkpoint/resume produced the only invalid run, and it did so in the interruption-sensitive resume condition. By contrast, dual evaluation, segment synthesis, and dynamic governance are best interpreted as supporting control mechanisms that shape trajectory discipline, explicit artifact progression, and cost-quality behavior rather than as universal binary prerequisites for completion. The contribution is therefore a knowledge-state orchestration architecture in which explicit epistemic state transition, evidence-bearing artifact progression, and recoverable continuity are the primary design commitments.

</details>


### [112] [Recursive Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25917)
*Xiyuan Yang,Jiaru Zou,Rui Pan,Ruizhong Qiu,Pan Lu,Shizhe Diao,Jindong Jiang,Hanghang Tong,Tong Zhang,Markus J. Buehler,Jingrui He,James Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RecursiveMAS框架，将递归计算从单模型扩展到多智能体系统，通过RecursiveLink模块实现跨智能体潜在空间状态传递，并设计内外环学习算法进行联合优化，在9个基准上平均提升8.3%准确率、1.2-2.4倍推理加速、34.6%-75.6% token缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索递归/循环语言模型从单一模型扩展到多智能体系统的可能性，即智能体协作本身能否通过递归方式进行规模化。

Method: 提出RecursiveMAS递归多智能体框架：(1) RecursiveLink模块连接异构智能体形成协作循环，实现潜在空间思维生成与跨智能体状态传递；(2) 内外环学习算法（inner-outer loop），通过共享梯度信用分配实现全系统联合优化。

Result: 在数学、科学、医学、搜索、代码生成等9个基准上，相比先进单/多智能体和递归计算基线，平均准确率提升8.3%，推理速度提升1.2-2.4倍，token使用量减少34.6%-75.6%。

Conclusion: 递归式多智能体协作是有效的规模化方向，RecursiveMAS在性能、效率、计算成本上均优于现有方法，证明了潜在空间递归计算在多智能体系统中的可行性。

Abstract: Recursive or looped language models have recently emerged as a new scaling axis by iteratively refining the same model computation over latent states to deepen reasoning. We extend such scaling principle from a single model to multi-agent systems, and ask: Can agent collaboration itself be scaled through recursion? To this end, we introduce RecursiveMAS, a recursive multi-agent framework that casts the entire system as a unified latent-space recursive computation. RecursiveMAS connects heterogeneous agents as a collaboration loop through the lightweight RecursiveLink module, enabling in-distribution latent thoughts generation and cross-agent latent state transfer. To optimize our framework, we develop an inner-outer loop learning algorithm for iterative whole-system co-optimization through shared gradient-based credit assignment across recursion rounds. Theoretical analyses of runtime complexity and learning dynamics establish that RecursiveMAS is more efficient than standard text-based MAS and maintains stable gradients during recursive training. Empirically, we instantiate RecursiveMAS under 4 representative agent collaboration patterns and evaluate across 9 benchmarks spanning mathematics, science, medicine, search, and code generation. In comparison with advanced single/multi-agent and recursive computation baselines, RecursiveMAS consistently delivers an average accuracy improvement of 8.3%, together with 1.2$\times$-2.4$\times$ end-to-end inference speedup, and 34.6%-75.6% token usage reduction. Code and Data are provided in https://recursivemas.github.io.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [113] [MotionBricks: Scalable Real-Time Motions with Modular Latent Generative Model and Smart Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24833)
*Tingwu Wang,Olivier Dionne,Michael De Ruyter,David Minor,Davis Rempe,Kaifeng Zhao,Mathis Petrovich,Ye Yuan,Chenran Li,Zhengyi Luo,Brian Robison,Xavier Blackwell,Bernardo Antoniazzi,Xue Bin Peng,Yuke Zhu,Simon Yuen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MotionBricks 是一个大规模、实时生成式运动框架，通过模块化潜空间生成骨干网络和智能原语接口，在实时约束下实现高质量运动生成，支持350,000+运动片段的多技能建模，达到15,000 FPS的实时吞吐量，并成功应用于动画制作和人形机器人控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生成式运动合成在实时交互控制方面面临两大挑战：1) 实时可扩展性——工业应用需要实时生成大量运动技能，但现有生成方法在实时计算约束下质量和可扩展性显著下降；2) 集成问题——工业应用需要细粒度的多模态控制（速度指令、风格选择、精确关键帧），而现有文本或标签驱动模型无法满足这些需求。

Method: MotionBricks 提出两阶段解决方案：1) 大规模模块化潜空间生成骨干网络，针对实时运动生成进行优化，使用单一模型对超过350,000个运动片段进行建模；2) 引入智能原语（smart primitives），提供统一、鲁棒且直观的接口，支持导航和物体交互的编排，应用可以像搭积木一样以即插即用方式设计。

Result: 在开源和专有数据集上达到了最先进的运动质量；实时吞吐量达到15,000 FPS，延迟仅2ms；在完整的工业级动画演示中展示了导航和物体-场景交互的灵活性与鲁棒性；成功部署到Unitree G1人形机器人上，验证了实时机器人控制的泛化能力。

Conclusion: MotionBricks 有效桥接了生成式运动合成研究与工业实际应用之间的差距，不仅为动画制作提供了即插即用的解决方案，还展示了超越传统动画领域、向实时机器人控制泛化的潜力。

Abstract: Despite transformative advances in generative motion synthesis, real-time interactive motion control remains dominated by traditional techniques. In this work, we identify two key challenges in bridging research and production: 1) Real-time scalability: Industry applications demand real-time generation of a vast repertoire of motion skills, while generative methods exhibit significant degradation in quality and scalability under real-time computation constraints, and 2) Integration: Industry applications demand fine-grained multi-modal control involving velocity commands, style selection, and precise keyframes, a need largely unmet by existing text- or tag-driven models. To overcome these limitations, we introduce MotionBricks: a large-scale, real-time generative framework with a two-fold solution. First, we propose a large-scale modular latent generative backbone tailored for robust real-time motion generation, effectively modeling a dataset of over 350,000 motion clips with a single model. Second, we introduce smart primitives that provide a unified, robust, and intuitive interface for authoring both navigation and object interaction. Applications can be designed in a plug-and-play manner like assembling bricks without expert animation knowledge. Quantitatively, we show that MotionBricks produces state-of-the-art motion quality on open-source and proprietary datasets of various scales, while also achieving a real-time throughput of 15,000 FPS with 2ms latency. We demonstrate the flexibility and robustness of MotionBricks in a complete production-level animation demo, covering navigation and object-scene interaction across various styles with a unified model. To showcase our framework's application beyond animation, we deploy MotionBricks on the Unitree G1 humanoid robot to demonstrate its flexibility and generalization for real-time robotic control.

</details>


### [114] [VISION-SLS: Safe Perception-Based Control from Learned Visual Representations via System Level Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24894)
*Antoine P. Leeman,Shuyu Zhan,Melanie N. Zeilinger,Glen Chou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出VISION-SLS方法，利用预训练视觉特征和系统级合成（SLS）实现高分辨率图像下的非线性输出反馈控制，在部分可观测、传感器噪声和非线性动力学条件下提供鲁棒约束满足保证。通过在视觉运动任务和硬件实验验证，该方法在安全性和求解时间上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图像的输出反馈控制方法在部分可观测、传感器噪声和非线性动力学条件下难以提供约束满足保证，且缺乏可扩展性。为解决这一问题，作者希望实现兼具鲁棒性保证与可扩展性的视觉反馈控制方法。

Method: (i) 从预训练视觉特征中学习低维观测映射并附带状态相关误差界；(ii) 通过系统级合成（SLS）优化因果仿射时变输出反馈策略；(iii) 采用顺序凸规划结合高效Riccati递归求解非凸优化问题。

Result: 在4D小车、10D四旋翼（≥512×512像素）、59D人形机器人等仿真任务以及地面车辆硬件实验中，该方法实现了安全的、信息收集行为，在降低不确定性的同时保证约束满足，安全率和求解时间均优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 学习得到的视觉抽象结合高效求解器使得基于SLS的安全视觉运动输出反馈控制达到实用规模，代码已开源。

Abstract: We propose VISION-SLS, a method for nonlinear output-feedback control from high-resolution RGB images which provides robust constraint satisfaction guarantees under calibrated uncertainty bounds despite partial observability, sensor noise, and nonlinear dynamics. To enable scalability while retaining guarantees, we propose: (i) a learned low-dimensional observation map from pretrained visual features with state-dependent error bounds, and (ii) a causal affine time-varying output-feedback policy optimized via System Level Synthesis (SLS). We develop a scalable, novel solver for the resulting nonconvex program that leverages sequential convex programming coupled with efficient Riccati recursions. On two simulated visuomotor tasks (a 4D car and a 10D quadrotor) with >= 512 x 512 pixels and a 59D humanoid task with partial observability, our method enables safe, information-gathering behavior that reduces uncertainty while guaranteeing constraint satisfaction with empirically-calibrated error bounds. We also validate our method on hardware, safely controlling a ground vehicle from onboard images, outperforming baselines in safety rate and solve times. Together, these results show that learned visual abstractions coupled with an efficient solver make SLS-based safe visuomotor output-feedback practical at scale. The code implementation of our method is available at https://github.com/trustworthyrobotics/VISION-SLS.

</details>


### [115] [An analysis of sensor selection for fruit picking with suction-based grippers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24906)
*Eva Krueger,Marcus Rosette,Joseph R. Davidson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文设计并评估了一种集成在柔性吸力式苹果夹爪中的多模态传感套件，通过相位依赖的传感器选择策略，实现了对水果采摘状态的高精度预测。实验表明，随机森林和多层感知器在真实果园中检测成功采摘和即将发生的失败准确率超过90%，随机森林预测拾取/滑移事件的时间误差仅为0.09秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人水果采摘中常常无法可靠检测水果是否被成功摘取，导致效率低下和作物损伤增加。现有方法面临水果和夹爪的柔性、茎干附着力的可变性以及果园环境中的遮挡等挑战。尽管已有基于视觉和多传感器学习的研究，但最小传感器集和相位依赖的感知策略仍然缺乏探索。

Method: 设计了一个多模态传感套件，集成在柔性吸力式苹果夹爪中，采用相位依赖的传感器评估策略，识别不同采摘阶段最具信息量的传感器。使用随机森林和多层感知器分类器进行采摘状态分类。

Result: 在真实苹果园实验中，随机森林和多层感知器检测成功采摘和即将发生的失败准确率超过90%，随机森林预测拾取/滑移事件的时间误差为0.09秒。

Conclusion: 本文证明了通过相位依赖的传感器选择和最小传感器集策略，可以在采摘过程中实现高精度的状态预测和早期失败检测，为提升机器人采摘的可靠性和效率提供了一种有效途径。

Abstract: Robotic fruit harvesting often fails to reliably detect whether a fruit has been successfully picked, limiting efficiency and increasing crop damage. This problem is difficult due to compliant fruit and grippers, variable stem attachment, and occlusions in orchard environments. Prior work has explored vision-based perception and multi-sensor learning approaches for pick state estimation. However, minimal sensor sets and phase-dependent sensing strategies for accurate pick and slip detection remain largely unexplored. In this work, we design and evaluate a multimodal sensing suite integrated into a compliant suction-based apple gripper. Our approach is unique because it identifies which sensors are most informative at different phases of the pick, enabling predictive detection of failures before they occur. The contributions of this paper are a phase-dependent evaluation of multimodal sensors and the identification of minimal sensor sets for reliable pick state classification. Experiments in a real apple orchard show that Random Forest and Multilayer Perceptron classifiers detect successful picks and impending failures with over 90% accuracy, and Random Forest predicts pick/slip events within 0.09 s of human-annotated ground truth.

</details>


### [116] [asRoBallet: Closing the Sim2Real Gap via Friction-Aware Reinforcement Learning for Underactuated Spherical Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24916)
*Fang Wan,Guangyi Huang,Tianyu Wu,Zishang Zhang,Bangchao Huang,Haoran Sun,Mingdong Chen,Chaoyang Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次在仿人滚球机器人硬件上成功部署强化学习(RL)，通过高保真MuJoCo仿真建模全向轮离散滚子力学，并提出摩擦感知RL框架实现零样本Sim2Real迁移，同时设计了低成本硬件平台和iOS交互系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前文献虽能用LQR和MPC处理3D平衡，但将RL迁移到实际仿人滚球机器人硬件时，存在接触建模、执行器延迟抖动和安全硬件探索方面的关键空白。

Method: 1) 高保真MuJoCo仿真，显式建模ETH型全向轮的离散滚子力学，捕捉寄生振动和接触不连续性；2) 摩擦感知RL框架，掌握轮-球和球-地面界面的耦合滚动、侧向和扭转摩擦通道，实现零样本Sim2Real迁移；3) 通过减法重构（重用过约束四足机器人组件）设计低成本硬件平台asRoBallet；4) 开发通用iOS生态系统，将消费电子产品转变为低延迟接口。

Result: 首次在仿人滚球机器人硬件上成功部署RL，实现了零样本Sim2Real迁移，并可通过iOS系统由单操作员以自然动作编排仿人操作。

Conclusion: 通过精细的物理仿真建模和摩擦感知RL框架，有效弥合了Sim2Real差距，为仿人滚球机器人的RL部署提供了可行的技术路线，且低成本硬件和直观交互方案具有实用价值。

Abstract: We introduce asRoBallet, to the best of our knowledge, the first successful deployment of reinforcement learning (RL) on a humanoid ballbot hardware. Historically, ballbots have served as a canonical benchmark for underactuated and nonholonomic control, which are characterized by a reality gap in complex friction models for wheel-sphere-ground interactions. While current literature demonstrates successful handling of 3D balancing with LQR and MPC, transitioning to actual hardware for a humanoid ballbot using RL is currently hindered by critical gaps in contact modeling, actuator latency & jitter, and safe hardware exploration, and safe hardware exploration. This study proposes a high-fidelity MuJoCo simulation that explicitly models the discrete roller mechanics of ETH-type omni-wheels, thereby capturing parasitic vibrations and contact discontinuities that are previously ignored. We also developed a Friction-Aware Reinforcement Learning framework that achieves zero-shot Sim2Real transfer by mastering the coupled rolling, lateral, and torsional friction channels at the wheel-sphere and sphere-ground interfaces. We designed asRoBallet through subtractive reconfiguration, repurposing key components from an overconstrained quadruped and integrating them into a newly designed structural frame to achieve a robust research platform at low cost. We also developed a generalized iOS ecosystem that transforms consumer electronics into a low-latency interface, enabling a single operator to orchestrate expressive humanoid maneuvers via intuitive natural motion.

</details>


### [117] [Libra-VLA: Achieving Learning Equilibrium via Asynchronous Coarse-to-Fine Dual-System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24921)
*Yifei Wei,Linqing Zhong,Yi Liu,Yuxiang Lu,Xindong He,Maoqing Yao,Guanghui Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Libra-VLA提出了一种粗到细的双系统VLA架构，通过将机器人操作解耦为语义规划器（离散宏动作）和动作精炼器（连续微动作），并采用异步执行策略，有效缩小了语义-执行差距，在动作分解粒度达到学习平衡时性能最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型采用扁平化、非层次化的整体生成范式，直接将视觉-语言特征映射到高频电机指令，忽视了机器人操作中固有的层次结构（混合动作空间），导致语义-执行差距扩大，高维连续动作给语义到动作的映射带来沉重的表示负担。

Method: 提出Libra-VLA粗到细双系统架构：(1) 语义规划器（Semantic Planner）预测离散动作token，捕捉宏观方向意图；(2) 动作精炼器（Action Refiner）基于粗意图生成高频连续动作，实现精确对齐。采用异步执行策略，并发现动作分解粒度与性能呈倒U型曲线关系。

Result: 性能与动作分解粒度呈倒U型曲线关系，当两个子系统间的学习难度达到平衡时性能最优。异步设计提供了可扩展、鲁棒且响应快速的开放世界操作解决方案。

Conclusion: 通过显式解耦学习复杂度为粗到细的层次结构并配合异步执行策略，Libra-VLA为开放世界机器人操作提供了一种可扩展且鲁棒的解决方案，验证了层次化动作分解对缩小语义-执行差距的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are a promising paradigm for generalist robotic manipulation by grounding high-level semantic instructions into executable physical actions. However, prevailing approaches typically adopt a monolithic generation paradigm, directly mapping visual-linguistic features to high-frequency motor commands in a flat, non-hierarchical fashion. This strategy overlooks the inherent hierarchy of robotic manipulation, where complex actions can be naturally modeled in a Hybrid Action Space, decomposing into discrete macro-directional reaching and continuous micro-pose alignment, severely widening the semantic-actuation gap and imposing a heavy representational burden on grounding high-level semantics to continuous actions. To address this, we introduce Libra-VLA, a novel Coarse-to-Fine Dual-System VLA architecture. We explicitly decouple the learning complexity into a coarse-to-fine hierarchy to strike a training equilibrium, while simultaneously leveraging this structural modularity to implement an asynchronous execution strategy. The Semantic Planner predicts discrete action tokens capturing macro-directional intent, while the Action Refiner conditions on coarse intent to generate high-frequency continuous actions for precise alignment. Crucially, our empirical analysis reveals that performance follows an inverted-U curve relative to action decomposition granularity, peaking exactly when the learning difficulty is balanced between the two sub-systems. With the asynchronous design, our approach offers a scalable, robust, and responsive solution for open-world manipulation.

</details>


### [118] [TEACar: An Open-Source Autonomous Driving Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24934)
*Zhongzheng Zhang,Maxwell Ruyle,Andrew Kappes,Tyler Ruble,William Shaoul,Dana Moreno,Jack Penn,Ivan Ruchkin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了TEACAR，一个1/14至1/16比例、基于ROS 2的模块化自动驾驶小规模平台，采用四层甲板结构解耦感知、计算、驱动和电源子系统，并通过三个CNN控制器验证了其机械稳定性、推理延迟和功耗等性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有小规模自动驾驶平台存在模块化不足、集成复杂度高或可扩展性受限的问题，难以满足ITS研究中硬件在环验证的需求。

Method: 采用四层甲板机械架构（感知、计算、驱动、电源分层独立），结合ROS 2软件框架与硬件抽象层，实现模块化解耦与快速重构。

Result: 基于三种CNN控制器完成了机械稳定性、结构特性、推理延迟、功耗及系统运行时间等量化评估。

Conclusion: TEACAR作为可扩展、模块化、低成本的测试平台，适用于ITS研究、教育与开发。

Abstract: Intelligent Transportation Systems (ITS) increasingly rely on vision-based perception and learning-based control, necessitating experimental platforms that support realistic hardware-in-the-loop validation. Small-scale platforms for autonomous racing offer a practical path to hardware validation, but often suffer from limited modularity, high integration complexity, or restricted extensibility. This paper presents TEACAR, a 1/14- to 1/16-scale autonomous driving platform designed with modular mechanical architecture, hardware abstraction, and ROS 2-based software. The system adopts a four-layer deck structure that physically decouples sensing, computation, actuation, and power subsystems, improving structural rigidity while simplifying reconfiguration. We constructed and comprehensively evaluated the prototype of TEACAR. Its mechanical stability, structural characteristics, and software performance were quantified based on three CNN-based steering controllers. Inference latency, power consumption, and system operating time were measured to evaluate computational capability and robustness. Our experiments demonstrated that TEACAR offers a scalable, modular, and cost-effective testbed for ITS research, education, and development. Our project repository is available on GitHub.

</details>


### [119] [DiscreteRTC: Discrete Diffusion Policies are Natural Asynchronous Executors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25050)
*Pengcheng Wang,Kaiwen Hong,Chensheng Peng,Katherine Driggs-Campbell,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Chenfeng Xu,Chen Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DiscreteRTC，利用离散扩散策略的原生修复能力替代流匹配策略的外部修正，实现异步执行（边思考边行动），在动态任务中取得更高成功率、更低延迟和零额外代码开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理AI系统必须边行动边思考，但同步执行器在块间停顿会导致动态任务失败。现有RTC基于流匹配策略存在结构缺陷：修复依赖推理时修正而非基础策略，需要特定微调、启发式指导且增加延迟。

Method: 提出DiscreteRTC，将离散扩散策略（通过迭代去掩码生成动作）作为异步执行器。利用其原生修复能力取代外部修正，支持早停策略实现自适应指导并降低推理成本。

Result: 在动态模拟基准和真实世界动态操作任务中，DiscreteRTC相比连续RTC和其他基线取得了更高成功率。实际动态抓取任务成功率提升50%，推理计算量仅为从头生成动作的0.7倍。

Conclusion: DiscreteRTC更简单（异步修复零代码修改）、更快（推理计算量减少30%）、更好（成功率提升50%），是物理AI异步执行的结构性优势方案。

Abstract: Unlike chatbots, physical AI must act while the world keeps evolving. Therefore, the inter-chunk pause of synchronous executors are fatal for dynamic tasks regardless of how fast the inference is. Asynchronous execution -- thinking while acting -- is therefore a structural requirement, and real-time chunking (RTC) makes it viable by recasting chunk transitions as inpainting: freezing committed actions and consistently generating the remainder. However, RTC with flow-matching policy is structurally suboptimal: its inpainting comes from inference-time corrections rather than the base policy, yielding little pre-training benefit, specific fine-tuning, heuristic guidance, and extra computation that inflates the latency. In this work, we observe that discrete diffusion policies, which generate actions by iteratively unmasking, are natural asynchronous executors that resolve all limitations at once: they are fine-tuning free since inpainting is their native operation, while early stopping further provides adaptive guidance and reduces inference cost. We propose DiscreteRTC, which replaces external corrections with native unmasking, and show on dynamic simulated benchmarks and real-world dynamic manipulation tasks that it achieves higher success rates than continuous RTC and other baselines. In summary, DiscreteRTC is simpler to implement with 0 lines of code for async inpainting, faster at inference with only 0.7x computation compared with generating actions from scratch, and better at execution with 50% higher success rate in real-world dynamic pick task compared with flow-matching-based RTC. More visualizations are on https://outsider86.github.io/DiscreteRTCSite/.

</details>


### [120] [HANDFUL: Sequential Grasp-Conditioned Dexterous Manipulation with Resource Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25126)
*Ethan Foong,Yunshuang Li,Hao Jiang,Gaurav S. Sukhatme,Daniel Seita*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出HANDFUL框架，将手指使用视为有限资源，通过手指级接触奖励鼓励资源感知抓取，并结合课程策略学习实现顺序灵巧操作任务，在仿真和真实实验中证明优于贪婪抓取基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵巧操作研究多聚焦于单物体、单技能任务，忽视了真实世界中机器人需要顺序执行多种操作（如先抓取再推/拉/按）的场景，而传统贪婪抓取策略会占用全部手指，导致后续子任务失败。

Method: 提出HANDFUL框架：1) 将手指使用建模为有限资源，设计手指级接触奖励引导探索资源感知抓取；2) 通过课程策略学习选择适合下游任务的抓取姿态；3) 构建HANDFUL-Bench仿真基准，包含推、拉、按等顺序灵巧操作任务。

Result: 仿真实验表明，资源感知抓取相比贪婪抓取基线显著提升了第二子任务的成功率和鲁棒性；在真实LEAP灵巧手上也验证了方法的可行性。

Conclusion: 资源感知的抓取规划是面向多功能灵巧操作的关键原则，为机器人顺序完成多技能操作提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Dexterous robot hands offer rich opportunities for multifunctional manipulation, where a robot must execute multiple skills in sequence while maintaining control over previously grasped objects. Most prior work in dexterous manipulation focuses on single-object, single-skill tasks. In contrast, our insight is that many sequential tasks require resource-aware grasps that conserve fingers for future actions. In this paper, we study sequential grasp-conditioned dexterous manipulation, where a robot first grasps an object and then performs a second, distinct manipulation subtask while preserving the initial grasp. We introduce HANDFUL, a learning framework that models finger usage as a limited resource and encourages exploration of resource-aware grasps through finger-level contact rewards. These grasps are subsequently selected for downstream tasks via curriculum-based policy learning. We further propose HANDFUL-Bench, a simulation benchmark that introduces sequential dexterous manipulation tasks across multiple secondsubtask objectives, including pushing, pulling, and pressing, under a shared grasp-conditioned setup. Extensive simulation results demonstrate that prioritizing resource-aware grasps improves second-subtask success and robustness compared to a baseline that greedily optimizes the initial grasp before attempting the second subtask. We additionally validate our approach on a real dexterous LEAP hand. Together, this work establishes resource-aware grasp planning as a key principle for multifunctional dexterous manipulation. Supplementary material is available on our website: https://handful-dex.github.io.

</details>


### [121] [Dynamic UGV-UAV Cooperative Path Planning in Uncertain Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25267)
*Ninh Nguyen,Srinivas Akella*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究动态UGV-UAV协同路径规划（DUCPP）问题，提出双向搜索等多种策略，使UGV在不确定路网中借助UAV动态检测边来找到安全路径。实验表明双向策略性能最优，多UAV可减少UGV行程时间但增加计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决灾难响应、紧急物资运输等场景中，UGV在部分未知道路条件下（可能存在不可通行边）需要安全高效到达目的地的问题，通过UAV-UAV协同来动态检测并排除受损或不可通行边。

Method: 提出了多种协同策略（包括双向搜索方法），让UAV动态检测环境中的边，识别并剔除受损/不可通行边；同时探索了多UAV对减少UGV行程时间的影响。

Result: 在100个城市路网上实验，双向策略在大多数实例中表现最优；使用多UAV可进一步降低UGV行程时间，但会带来计算时间的增加。

Conclusion: 该论文为DUCPP提供了鲁棒的框架，实现了UGV与UAV在路径规划和检测方面的高效协同，为在不确定挑战条件下的导航提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: This paper addresses the Dynamic UGV-UAV Cooperative Path Planning (DUCPP) problem involving one unmanned ground vehicle (UGV) assisted by one or more unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) operating on an uncertain road network with potentially impassable edges. DUCPP is particularly relevant for scenarios such as disaster response, emergency supply transport, and rescue operations, where a UGV must reach a specified destination in the presence of partially unknown road conditions. To enable the UGV to travel safely and efficiently to its destination, the UAV(s) dynamically inspect edges in the environment to identify and prune damaged or impassable edges from consideration.
  We present multiple strategies, including a bidirectional approach, to optimize UGV-UAV cooperation for finding a safe path in an uncertain road network. Furthermore, we explore the impact of using multiple UAVs on reducing the UGV's travel time, and evaluate the associated computation time. The proposed strategies are implemented and evaluated on 100 urban road networks. The results demonstrate that the bidirectional strategy achieves the best performance in most instances, and using multiple UAVs further reduces UGV travel time at the expense of increased computation time. This paper presents a robust framework for DUCPP to achieve efficient UGV-UAV cooperation for path planning and inspection, offering practical solutions for navigation in challenging and uncertain conditions.

</details>


### [122] [Optimal UGV-UAV Cooperative Partitioning and Inspection of Shortest Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25284)
*Ninh Nguyen,Srinivas Akella*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究UGV与UAV协作的未知障碍最短路径规划问题，提出路径分区策略，在50个城市道路网络实验中实现UGV行程时间最多降低30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CTP假设单地面车辆且节点处才揭示边状态，本文将其推广到空地协作场景，旨在利用UAV的空中优势提前探测未知障碍，优化UGV的路径规划效率。

Method: 理论分析k条不相交路径下的竞争比；提出最优路径分区策略（UGV负责前缀检查、UAV负责后缀检查）；在50个城市道路网络上进行随机阻塞实验验证。

Result: 单UGV的竞争比为2k-1；有UAV辅助时竞争比优化为2(v_G/(v_A+v_G))k-1；所提方法在50个城市实验中UGV行程时间降低最多30%。

Conclusion: UAV辅助能显著提升UGV在未知障碍环境下的路径规划效率，所提出的分区策略在一般图上具有最优性。

Abstract: We study cooperative shortest path planning for an unmanned ground vehicle (UGV) assisted by an unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) in environments with unknown road blockages that are only discovered when a robot reaches the damaged point. This formulation generalizes the original Canadian Traveller Problem (CTP), which assumes a single ground vehicle and that the traversability status of all incident edges is revealed upon arrival at a vertex. We first analyze the case where the start and the goal are connected by $k$ disjoint paths, and prove that the worst-case competitive ratio $ρ$ for a single UGV is $2k-1$. With UAV assistance, and under the simplifying assumption of negligible initial transit and deadheading UAV costs, the ratio improves to $ρ= 2\frac{v_G}{v_A + v_G}k - 1$, where $v_G$ and $v_A$ denote the UGV and UAV speed, respectively. To address general graphs and non-negligible UAV initial transit and deadheading costs, we present an optimal path partitioning strategy that assigns path prefix inspection to the UGV and path suffix inspection to the UAV, and prove the optimality of the UAV inspection strategy on general graphs. We evaluate our algorithm by performing experiments on road networks from the world's 50 most populous cities, with randomized blockages, and show that the proposed method reduces UGV travel times by up to 30%.

</details>


### [123] [Slot-hopping Enabled Loiter Guidance and Automation for Fixed-wing UAV Corridors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25292)
*Pradeep J,Siddhardha Kedarisetty,Ashwini Ratnoo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对固定翼无人机走廊的交通拥堵管理问题，提出了一种半协同导引策略，使进入等待车道的无人机通过速度调整与空槽会合，或在必要时通过少量无人机协同跳槽来创建空槽，从而实现紧凑型固定翼无人机走廊的低干扰插入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有固定翼无人机走廊存在交通拥堵问题，需要更紧凑的走廊设计，同时要尽量减少对已在车道内运行的无人机的干扰。

Method: 提出半协同导引策略：进入无人机首先尝试在速度限制内与现有空槽会合；若直接插入不可行，则让少量等待无人机执行协调跳槽以创建合适位置的空槽。

Result: 通过数值仿真验证了该方法的可行性和性能。

Conclusion: 该半协同策略能够在实现紧凑走廊的同时，最小化对已有无人机的干扰，有效管理固定翼无人机走廊的交通拥堵。

Abstract: This paper addresses the problem of traffic congestion management in fixed-wing unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) corridors by further developing a recently introduced loiter-lane framework. A semi-cooperative guidance strategy is developed for inserting fixed-wing UAVs into a loiter lane with minimal disruption to the UAVs already operating within it, while enabling a more compact fixed-wing UAV corridor. Building on the concepts of cooperative and non-disruptive loiter-lane insertion, the proposed strategy makes the incoming UAV first attempt, within its speed bounds, to rendezvous with an existing empty loiter slot. If direct insertion is infeasible, a minimal number of loitering UAVs perform coordinated slot hopping to create a suitably positioned empty slot. The feasibility and performance of the method are demonstrated through numerical simulations.

</details>


### [124] [ANCHOR: A Physically Grounded Closed-Loop Framework for Robust Home-Service Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25323)
*Jinhao Jiang,Shengyu Fang,Sibo Zuo,Yujie Tang,Yirui Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ANCHOR是一个物理闭环框架，通过物理锚定任务规划、可操作性感知的基座对齐和最小责任层分层恢复三种机制，解决开放词汇移动操作中符号计划与物理世界不一致的问题，在60次真实机器人实验中任务成功率从53.3%提升至71.7%，扰动下恢复率达71.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇移动操作系统在真实家庭环境中存在三类关键局限：依赖预扫描语义地图导致场景变化后不一致、导航终点选择不考虑下游操作可行性导致"到达但无法操作"、异常处理通过无差别的全局重规划无法有效控制局部错误。这些失败的根本原因是符号计划与演化的物理世界之间的不一致。

Method: 提出ANCHOR框架，包含三个核心机制：(1) 物理锚定任务规划——将符号谓词绑定到可观测的几何锚点，并在每个动作后重新验证；(2) 可操作性感知基座对齐——确保导航终点满足运动学可达性和局部碰撞可行性；(3) 最小责任层分层恢复——在感知层、基座-手臂协调层和执行层定位故障，防止级联重试。

Result: 在60次真实机器人实验中，任务成功率从53.3%提升至71.7%，扰动下恢复率达71.4%。

Conclusion: 明确的物理锚定和结构化的故障隔离对于鲁棒的移动操作至关重要，验证了将符号推理与可验证的物理状态对齐能够有效解决执行不一致问题。

Abstract: Recent advances in open-vocabulary mobile manipulation have brought robots into real domestic environments. In such settings, reliable long-horizon execution under open-set object references and frequent disturbances becomes essential. However, many failures persist. These are not caused by semantic misunderstanding but by inconsistencies between symbolic plans and the evolving physical world, manifested as three recurring limitations: (i) existing systems often rely on pre-scanned semantic maps that become inconsistent after scene changes and disturbances; (ii) they select navigation endpoints without considering downstream manipulation feasibility, causing the "arrived but inoperable" problem; and (iii) they handle anomalies through undifferentiated global replanning, which often fails to contain local errors. To address this execution inconsistency, we present ANCHOR, a physically grounded closed-loop framework that aligns symbolic reasoning with verifiable physical state during execution. ANCHOR integrates three mechanisms: (i) physically anchored task planning, which binds symbolic predicates to observable geometric anchors and re-validates them after each action; (ii) operability-aware base alignment, which ensures that navigation endpoints satisfy kinematic reachability and local collision feasibility; and (iii) minimum-responsible-layer hierarchical recovery, which localizes failures across perception, base-arm coordination, and execution layers to prevent cascading retries. Across 60 real-robot trials in previously unseen environments, ANCHOR improves task success from 53.3% to 71.7% and achieves a 71.4% recovery rate under perturbations, demonstrating that explicit physical grounding and structured failure containment are critical for robust mobile manipulation. Our project page is available at https://anchor9178.github.io/ANCHOR/ .

</details>


### [125] [ProDrive: Proactive Planning for Autonomous Driving via Ego-Environment Co-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25329)
*Chuyao Fu,Shengzhe Gan,Zhuoli Ouyang,Yuhan Rui,Xiaowei Chi,Sirui Han,Jiankun Wang,Hong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ProDrive是一个基于世界模型的端到端主动规划框架，通过联合训练查询轨迹规划器和BEV世界模型，实现自主驾驶中自我-环境协同演化，优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端自动驾驶规划器仅依赖当前观测生成轨迹，无法预测未来场景变化，导致短视决策和安全隐患，亟需能够预见未来演化的主动规划方法。

Method: 联合训练查询中心轨迹规划器（生成候选轨迹和规划感知自我令牌）与BEV世界模型（预测未来场景演化），通过双向耦合实现规划特征注入世界模型、并行评估所有候选轨迹并保持端到端梯度流动。

Result: 在NAVSIM v1上，ProDrive在安全性和规划效率上均优于强基线方法，消融实验验证了自我-环境耦合设计的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过双向耦合的自我-环境共同演化机制，ProDrive实现了超越当前观测驱动的主动规划，为自动驾驶的安全决策提供了新范式。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving planners typically generate trajectories from current observations alone. However, real-world driving is highly dynamic, and such reactive planning cannot anticipate future scene evolution, often leading to myopic decisions and safety-critical failures. We propose ProDrive, a world-model-based proactive planning framework that enables ego-environment co-evolution for autonomous driving. ProDrive jointly trains a query-centric trajectory planner and a bird's-eye-view (BEV) world model end-to-end: the planner generates diverse candidate trajectories and planning-aware ego tokens, while the world model predicts future scene evolution conditioned on them. By injecting planner features into the world model and evaluating all candidates in parallel, ProDrive preserves end-to-end gradient flow and allows future outcome assessment to directly shape planning. This bidirectional coupling enables proactive planning beyond current-observation-driven decision-making. Experiments on NAVSIM v1 show that ProDrive outperforms strong baselines in both safety and planning efficiency, while ablations validate the effectiveness of the proposed ego-environment coupling design.

</details>


### [126] [Robust Graph Matching through Semantic Relationship Generation for SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25404)
*David Perez-Saura,Jose Andres Millan-Romera,Miguel Fernandez-Cortizas,Holger Voos,Pascual Campoy,Jose Luis Sanchez-Lopez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种语义增强的图匹配方法，通过显式建模检测物体与结构元素（如房间、墙面）之间的关系，在重复或对称布局环境中显著减少匹配歧义，提升计算效率和收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在重复或对称布局的室内环境中，仅依赖结构线索进行场景图匹配往往难以解决歧义问题。

Method: 从RGB-D数据中检测物体并融入图中，利用物体与结构元素（房间、墙面等）的关系过滤候选对应关系，再进行几何验证。

Result: 语义关系显著减少了候选匹配数量，提高了计算效率，并在对称场景中实现了更快的收敛。

Conclusion: 语义增强的图匹配方法有效解决了纯几何方法在对称/重复布局中的失败问题，提升了定位鲁棒性和效率。

Abstract: Graph-based representations such as Scene Graphs enable localization in structured indoor environments by matching a locally observed graph, constructed from sensor data, to a prior map. This process is particularly challenging in environments with repetitive or symmetric layouts, where structural cues alone are often insufficient to resolve ambiguities. We propose a semantic-enhanced graph matching approach that explicitly models relations between detected objects and structural elements, such as rooms and wall planes. Objects are detected from RGB-D data and integrated into the graph, and their relations to structural elements are exploited to filter candidate correspondences prior to geometric verification, significantly reducing ambiguity and search complexity. The proposed method is integrated within the iS-Graphs framework and evaluated in synthetic and simulated environments. Results show that semantic relations significantly reduce the number of candidate matches, improve computational efficiency, and enable faster convergence, particularly in symmetric scenarios where purely geometric approaches fail.

</details>


### [127] [GS-Playground: A High-Throughput Photorealistic Simulator for Vision-Informed Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25459)
*Yufei Jia,Heng Zhang,Ziheng Zhang,Junzhe Wu,Mingrui Yu,Zifan Wang,Dixuan Jiang,Zheng Li,Chenyu Cao,Zhuoyuan Yu,Xun Yang,Haizhou Ge,Yuchi Zhang,Jiayuan Zhang,Zhenbiao Huang,Tianle Liu,Shenyu Chen,Jiacheng Wang,Bin Xie,Xuran Yao,Xiwa Deng,Guangyu Wang,Jinzhi Zhang,Lei Hao,Zhixing Chen,Yuxiang Chen,Anqi Wang,Hongyun Tian,Yiyi Yan,Zhanxiang Cao,Yizhou Jiang,Hanyang Shao,Yue Li,Lu Shi,Bokui Chen,Wei Sui,Hanqing Cui,Yusen Qin,Ruqi Huang,Lei Han,Tiancai Wang,Guyue Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GS-Playground是一个多模态仿真框架，通过将批量3D高斯泼溅渲染与高性能物理引擎结合，实现10^4 FPS的高效渲染，并引入自动Real2Sim工作流，有效降低视觉强化学习门槛，弥合感知与物理差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仿真器在视觉感知任务中面临三大瓶颈：大规模逼真渲染的计算开销过高、仿真3D资产依赖人工建模、以及sim-to-real物理差距阻碍操控策略迁移。亟需一个高效、高保真且自动化的仿真框架。

Method: 提出GS-Playground框架，包含：(1) 高性能并行物理引擎，与批量3D高斯泼溅(3DGS)渲染管线集成，实现高保真同步；(2) 自动Real2Sim工作流，重建逼真、物理一致且内存高效的仿真环境。

Result: 在640x480分辨率下实现10^4 FPS的突破性吞吐量，显著降低大规模视觉强化学习门槛；在 locomotion、navigation、manipulation等任务上验证了框架能有效弥合感知与物理差距。

Conclusion: GS-Playground通过高效的渲染-物理协同框架和自动化场景生成，为具身AI的视觉感知学习提供了可扩展的解决方案，有望加速端到端感知策略的研究。

Abstract: Embodied AI research is undergoing a shift toward vision-centric perceptual paradigms. While massively parallel simulators have catalyzed breakthroughs in proprioception-based locomotion, their potential remains largely untapped for vision-informed tasks due to the prohibitive computational overhead of large-scale photorealistic rendering. Furthermore, the creation of simulation-ready 3D assets heavily relies on labor-intensive manual modeling, while the significant sim-to-real physical gap hinders the transfer of contact-rich manipulation policies. To address these bottlenecks, we propose GS-Playground, a multi-modal simulation framework designed to accelerate end-to-end perceptual learning. We develop a novel high-performance parallel physics engine, specifically designed to integrate with a batch 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) rendering pipeline to ensure high-fidelity synchronization. Our system achieves a breakthrough throughput of 10^4 FPS at 640x480 resolution, significantly lowering the barrier for large-scale visual RL. Additionally, we introduce an automated Real2Sim workflow that reconstructs photorealistic, physically consistent, and memory-efficient environments, streamlining the generation of complex simulation-ready scenes. Extensive experiments on locomotion, navigation, and manipulation demonstrate that GS-Playground effectively bridges the perceptual and physical gaps across diverse embodied tasks. Project homepage: https://gsplayground.github.io.

</details>


### [128] [Egocentric Tactile and Proximity Sensors as Observation Priors for Humanoid Collision Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25554)
*Carson Kohlbrenner,Niraj Pudasaini,William Xie,Naren Sivagnanadasan,Nikolaus Correll,Alessandro Roncone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究使用强化学习框架，在类人机器人H1-2上探究了触觉和接近传感器特性（覆盖范围、类型、距离）如何影响全身避碰行为，发现原始接近测量值可替代显式目标定位，且稀疏非方向性信号比密集方向性信号更具样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于外部摄像头容易受到遮挡，机器人常依赖身体上分布的触觉和接近传感器进行无碰撞运动，但传感器的特性（如覆盖范围、类型、距离）如何影响避让行为尚不明确。

Method: 使用强化学习框架，以躲避球为基准任务，在类人机器人H1-2上进行全身避碰研究，通过消融实验分析分布在上半身的传感器属性。

Result: 原始接近测量值在传感距离足够时可替代显式目标定位；稀疏非方向性接近信号在样本效率上优于密集方向性信号。

Conclusion: 传感器的距离、类型和方向性等特性显著影响学习到的避碰行为，选择合适的传感器配置可提升避碰性能。

Abstract: Collision-free motion is often aided by tactile and proximity sensors distributed on the body of the robot due to their resistance to occlusion as opposed to external cameras. However, how to shape the sensor's properties, such as sensing coverage; type; and range, to enable avoidant behavior remains unclear. In this work, we present a reinforcement learning framework for whole-body collision avoidance on a humanoid H1-2 robot and use it to characterize how sensor properties shape learned avoidance behavior. Using dodgeball as a benchmark task, we ablate the properties of sensors distributed across the upper body of the robot and find that raw proximity measurements can substitute for explicit object localization provided the sensing range is sufficient and that sparse non-directional proximity signals outpace dense directional alternatives in sample efficiency.

</details>


### [129] [Improving Sensing Coverage and Compliance of 3D-Printed Artificial Skins Through Multi-Modal Sensing and Soft Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25563)
*Carson Kohlbrenner,Caleb Escobedo,Sayak Ray,Alexander Dickhans,Anna Soukhovei,Nickolaus Jackoski,Lyle Antieau,Alessandro Roncone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种3D打印混合飞行时间与自电容传感的人造皮肤，实现了多模态感知、软性覆盖和简化电气接口，并在机器人手臂上部署了6个单元40个传感元件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D打印人造皮肤局限于单模态感知和刚性材料，实用性和可扩展性不足。

Method: 混合ToF和SC传感技术，结合软性覆盖材料，优化印刷导电迹线与外部电气的接口设计。

Result: 在FR3机器人手臂上部署6个人造皮肤单元（40个传感元件），实现了接触检测、场景重建和压力相关的触觉响应。

Conclusion: 该混合传感皮肤有效提升了3D打印人造皮肤的多模态能力、柔软性和电气集成度，展示了在实际机器人系统中的应用潜力。

Abstract: 3D-printed artificial skins are a scalable approach to whole-body tactile and proximity coverage, but prior implementations have been limited to unimodal sensing and rigid materials. To improve the practical usability of 3D-printed artificial skins, we present a hybrid time-of-flight (ToF) and self-capacitance (SC) sensing skin that demonstrates multi-modal sensing integration, soft compliant coverings for impact absorption and pressure sensing, and a streamlined electrical interface between printed conductive traces and external electronics. We show that combining ToF and SC modalities enables contact detection, scene reconstruction, and pressure-correlated tactile responses with the compliant covering by deploying six artificial skin units with 40 sensing elements over an FR3 robot arm.

</details>


### [130] [SlicerRoboTMS: An Open-Source 3D Slicer Extension for Robot-Assisted Transcranial Magnetic Stimulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25661)
*Wenzhi Bai,Yituo Guo,Bhaskar Basu,Andrew Weightman,Zhenhong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了SlicerRoboTMS，一个基于3D Slicer的开源扩展，为机器人辅助经颅磁刺激（Robo-TMS）提供统一的交互基础设施，支持MRI导航和机器人系统集成，降低了该领域的研究门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Robo-TMS开发需要多学科专业知识（医学影像、计算机视觉、机器人），缺乏统一的工具平台，导致开发门槛高、可重复性差。

Method: 基于3D Slicer开发开源扩展，利用其医学影像计算与可视化能力，支持MRI导航，通过标准化通信协议和可配置系统描述与机器人系统交互。

Result: 提供了一个示例集成流程，展示了SlicerRoboTMS在典型Robo-TMS工作流中的应用。扩展已在GitHub开源发布。

Conclusion: SlicerRoboTMS降低了Robo-TMS研究门槛，支持多样化硬件配置和快速原型开发，促进了该领域的可重复和可扩展研究。

Abstract: Robot-assisted Transcranial Magnetic Stimulation (Robo-TMS) is an image-guided robotic intervention that enhances the accuracy and reproducibility of conventional Transcranial Magnetic Stimulation (TMS), a widely used non-invasive brain stimulation procedure in clinical treatment and neuroscience research. Despite its potential, the development of Robo-TMS remains challenging due to the need for multidisciplinary expertise spanning medical imaging, computer vision, and robotics. This paper presents SlicerRoboTMS, an open-source 3D Slicer extension that provides a unified interaction infrastructure for Robo-TMS research. By leveraging 3D Slicer's medical image computing and visualisation capabilities, the extension supports Magnetic Resonance Imaging (MRI)-based neuronavigation and interfaces with robotic systems through standardised communication protocols and configurable system descriptions. An example integration is presented to demonstrate how SlicerRoboTMS can be incorporated into a representative Robo-TMS workflow. Designed to support diverse hardware configurations and rapid prototyping, SlicerRoboTMS lowers the barrier to entry and facilitates reproducible and extensible research in Robo-TMS. The extension is available at https://github.com/OpenRoboTMS/SlicerRoboTMS.

</details>


### [131] [GEGLU-Transformer for IMU-to-EMG Estimation with Few-Shot Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25670)
*Miroljub Mihailovic,Luca Tonin,Stefano Tortora,Emanuele Menegatti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自适应IMU-to-EMG学习框架，利用Transformer与GEGLU模块从惯性测量数据重建肌肉激活信号，在少样本适应下达到高相关性表现，为可穿戴机器人提供无需直接EMG传感的实用方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表面EMG信号在实验室外部署困难，存在电极敏感、信号非平稳及强个体依赖性问题，亟需一种鲁棒且可泛化的替代方案来估计神经肌肉激活，用于可穿戴机器人的自适应控制。

Method: 提出GEGLU-Transformer架构，结合Transformer编码器与高斯误差门控线性单元，从IMU测量数据重建连续肌肉激活包络；采用留一受试者验证（LOSO）协议，并利用仅0.5%的少量数据进行快速个性化适应。

Result: LOSO条件下r=0.706±0.139, R²=0.474±0.208；使用0.5%适应数据后提升至r=0.761±0.030, R²=0.559±0.047，表现出快速适应与早期性能饱和特性。

Conclusion: 基于注意力的架构结合轻量级适应策略，可作为直接EMG传感的实用且可扩展的替代方案，适用于真实世界的可穿戴机器人应用。

Abstract: Reliable estimation of neuromuscular activation is a key enabler for adaptive and personalized control in wearable robotics. However, surface electromyography (EMG) remains difficult to deploy robustly outside laboratory settings due to electrode sensitivity, signal non-stationarity, and strong subject dependence. In this work, we propose an adaptive IMU-to-EMG learning framework that reconstructs continuous muscle activation envelopes from wearable inertial measurements across heterogeneous movement conditions. The approach combines a Transformer encoder with Gaussian Error Gated Linear Units (GEGLU-Transformer) to enhance cross-subject generalization and enable rapid subject-specific personalization. Under a strict leave-one-subject-out (LOSO) protocol on a multi-condition lower-limb biomechanics dataset, the proposed architecture achieves r = 0.706 +/- 0.139 and R^2 = 0.474 +/- 0.208 without subject-specific adaptation. With only 0.5% adaptation data, performance increases to r = 0.761 +/- 0.030 and R^2 = 0.559 +/- 0.047, demonstrating rapid adaptation and early performance saturation. These results support attention-based architectures combined with lightweight adaptation as a practical and scalable alternative to direct EMG sensing for real-world wearable robotic applications.

</details>


### [132] [Learning-Based Dynamics Modeling and Robust Control for Tendon-Driven Continuum Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25691)
*Ziqing Zou,Ke Qiu,Fei Wang,Haojian Lu,Rong Xiong,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对肌腱驱动连续体机器人的非线性建模与控制难题，提出了一种可微学习框架，结合GRU动力学模型与端到端神经控制策略，在物理实验中实现了高精度跟踪并对未知负载具有强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肌腱驱动连续体机器人（TDCRs）由于摩擦滞后、传动柔顺等复杂非线性特性，传统建模与控制方法面临精度不足和稳定性问题（如自激振荡）。

Method: 提出可微学习框架：(1) 基于GRU的双向多通道连接与残差预测的动力学模型，抑制长时自回归预测中的误差累积；(2) 将动力学模型作为梯度桥梁，通过反向传播优化端到端神经控制策略，隐式补偿复杂非线性。

Result: 在三段式TDCR物理平台上验证，实现了精确轨迹跟踪，对未知负载表现出强鲁棒性，且相比雅可比方法消除了自激振荡。

Conclusion: 该可微学习框架有效解决了TDCR的非线性建模与控制难题，通过数据驱动方式隐式学习非线性补偿，为连续体机器人的精准控制提供了新范式。

Abstract: Tendon-Driven Continuum Robots (TDCRs) pose significant modeling and control challenges due to complex nonlinearities, such as frictional hysteresis and transmission compliance. This paper proposes a differentiable learning framework that integrates high-fidelity dynamics modeling with robust neural control. We develop a GRU-based dynamics model featuring bidirectional multi-channel connectivity and residual prediction to effectively suppress compounding errors during long-horizon auto-regressive prediction. By treating this model as a gradient bridge, an end-to-end neural control policy is optimized through backpropagation, allowing it to implicitly internalize compensation for intricate nonlinearities. Experimental validation on a physical three-section TDCR demonstrates that our framework achieves accurate tracking and superior robustness against unseen payloads, outperforming Jacobian-based methods by eliminating self-excited oscillations.

</details>


### [133] [Reference-Augmented Learning for Precise Tracking Policy of Tendon-Driven Continuum Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25698)
*Ziqing Zou,Ke Qiu,Haojian Lu,Rong Xiong,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对肌腱驱动连续体机器人(TDCR)的非线性和非马尔可夫控制难题，提出一种参考增强的离线学习框架，利用可微RNN动力学代理作为梯度桥梁，通过多尺度增强分布（随机偏差、谐波扰动、随机游走）优化控制策略，在6-DOF跟踪控制中实现50.9%的位置误差降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TDCR存在高度非线性、路径依赖性和非马尔可夫特性，传统雅可比控制器易受迟滞振荡困扰，而纯学习方法对分布外轨迹泛化能力差，亟需一种不依赖额外硬件交互的高精度控制框架。

Method: 提出参考增强的离线学习框架：1) 构建可微RNN动力学代理作为梯度桥梁；2) 设计多尺度参考增强分布，包含随机偏差、谐波扰动和随机游走三种扰动机制；3) 通过增强分布优化控制策略，使其内化多样的跟踪误差恢复机制。

Result: 在三段式TDCR平台上，所提策略相比非增强基线降低50.9%的平均位置误差，且在多种速度下精度和稳定性均显著优于雅可比方法。

Conclusion: 参考增强的离线学习范式有效解决了TDCR的复杂控制问题，多尺度扰动策略在不增加硬件交互的前提下显著提升了策略的泛化能力和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Tendon-Driven Continuum Robots (TDCRs) pose significant control challenges due to their highly nonlinear, path-dependent dynamics and non-Markovian characteristics. Traditional Jacobian-based controllers often struggle with hysteresis-induced oscillations, while conventional learning-based approaches suffer from poor generalization to out-of-distribution trajectories. This paper proposes a reference-augmented offline learning framework for precise 6-DOF tracking control of TDCRs. By leveraging a differentiable RNN-based dynamics surrogate as a gradient bridge, we optimize a control policy through an augmented reference distribution. This multi-scale augmentation scheme incorporates stochastic bias, harmonic perturbations, and random walks, forcing the policy to internalize diverse tracking error recovery mechanisms without additional hardware interaction. Experimental results on a three-section TDCR platform demonstrate that the proposed policy achieves a 50.9\% reduction in average position error compared to non-augmented baselines and significantly outperforms Jacobian-based methods in both precision and stability across various speeds.

</details>


### [134] [Sensitivity-Based Tube NMPC for Cooperative Aerial Structures Under Parametric Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25766)
*Giuseppe Silano,Quentin Sablé,Marco Tognon,Luigi Iannelli,Antonio Franchi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于灵敏度的管式NMPC框架，用于参数不确定性下的协同空中链控制，通过传播一阶参数状态灵敏度在线收紧约束，在MATLAB中验证了边界逼近机动和蒙特卡洛采样下的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决双飞行器刚性连接链在参数不确定性（连杆质量、长度、惯性）下的鲁棒控制问题，确保约束满足同时保持跟踪性能。

Method: 采用基于灵敏度的管式NMPC方法，沿预测时域传播一阶参数状态灵敏度，在线计算约束收紧裕度；对链间分离约束采用光滑余弦嵌入实现鲁棒化，并鲁棒化推力幅值约束。

Result: 在MATLAB中通过边界逼近机动和蒙特卡洛不确定性采样验证，表明在不确定性下约束裕度得到改善，跟踪性能与标称NMPC相当。

Conclusion: 所提框架能有效应对参数不确定性，在保证约束满足的同时维持良好跟踪性能，适用于协同空中链系统。

Abstract: This paper presents a sensitivity-based tube Nonlinear Model Predictive Control (NMPC) framework for cooperative aerial chains under bounded parametric uncertainty. We consider a planar two-vehicle chain connected by rigid links, modeled with input-rate actuation to enforce slew-rate and magnitude limits on thrust and torque. Robustness to uncertainty in link mass, length, and inertia is achieved by propagating first-order parametric state sensitivities along the horizon and using them to compute online constraint-tightening margins. We robustify an inter-link separation constraint, implemented via a smooth cosine embedding, and thrust-magnitude bounds. The method is implemented in MATLAB and evaluated with boundary-hugging maneuvers and Monte-Carlo uncertainty sampling. Results show improved constraint margins under uncertainty with tracking performance comparable to nominal NMPC.

</details>


### [135] [KinDER: A Physical Reasoning Benchmark for Robot Learning and Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25788)
*Yixuan Huang,Bowen Li,Vaibhav Saxena,Yichao Liang,Utkarsh Aashu Mishra,Liang Ji,Lihan Zha,Jimmy Wu,Nishanth Kumar,Sebastian Scherer,Danfei Xu,Tom Silver*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: KinDER是一个面向机器人物理推理的基准测试，包含25个环境、13个基线方法，覆盖5类核心物理推理挑战，实验表明现有方法存在明显不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人系统需要推理运动学和动力学约束，但目前缺乏系统性的基准来评估不同方法在物理推理方面的能力。

Method: 构建了25个程序化生成的环境，包含5类物理推理挑战（空间关系、非抓取多物体操作、工具使用、组合几何约束、动力学约束），集成了13种基线方法（任务与运动规划、模仿学习、强化学习、基础模型方法），并提供Gymnasium兼容库和真实-仿真-真实实验。

Result: 现有方法（包括任务规划、模仿学习、强化学习、基础模型方法）在多数环境下表现不佳，揭示了当前物理推理方法的显著不足。

Conclusion: KinDER为机器人物理推理提供了系统化的评估平台，有助于推动该领域的发展，代码已完全开源。

Abstract: Robotic systems that interact with the physical world must reason about kinematic and dynamic constraints imposed by their own embodiment, their environment, and the task at hand. We introduce KinDER, a benchmark for Kinematic and Dynamic Embodied Reasoning that targets physical reasoning challenges arising in robot learning and planning. KinDER comprises 25 procedurally generated environments, a Gymnasium-compatible Python library with parameterized skills and demonstrations, and a standardized evaluation suite with 13 implemented baselines spanning task and motion planning, imitation learning, reinforcement learning, and foundation-model-based approaches. The environments are designed to isolate five core physical reasoning challenges: basic spatial relations, nonprehensile multi-object manipulation, tool use, combinatorial geometric constraints, and dynamic constraints, disentangled from perception, language understanding, and application-specific complexity. Empirical evaluation shows that existing methods struggle to solve many of the environments, indicating substantial gaps in current approaches to physical reasoning. We additionally include real-to-sim-to-real experiments on a mobile manipulator to assess the correspondence between simulation and real-world physical interaction. KinDER is fully open-sourced and intended to enable systematic comparison across diverse paradigms for advancing physical reasoning in robotics. Website and code: https://prpl-group.com/kinder-site/

</details>


### [136] [Privileged Foresight Distillation: Zero-Cost Future Correction for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25859)
*Pengcheng Fang,Hongli Chen,Xiaohao Cai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出“特权未来蒸馏（PFD）”方法，将未来信息视为动作去噪的残差修正，从联合训练的世界动作模型中蒸馏到仅使用当前帧的轻量适配器，在保持纯当前推理接口的同时提升操作任务性能，未来视频在推理时无需生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型在训练时联合预测未来视频和动作，但测试时可移除未来预测分支且性能损失极小。作者质疑“未来信息仅作为正则项”的观点，提出未来信息实际上提供了一种动作条件化的修正信号，当前策略只能部分捕获这一修正。

Method: 将特权未来信息形式化为动作去噪方向的残差（教师使用真实未来帧，学生仅用当前帧），提出PFD方法，通过注意力掩码控制，将残差从教师蒸馏到学生的轻量适配器中。教师和学生共享同一视觉主干，推理时无需未来视频。

Result: 在LIBERO和RoboTwin操作基准上取得一致的性能提升，消融实验验证了增益来源于未来条件修正本身，而非容量增加或正则化副作用。推理延迟几乎无增加。

Conclusion: 重新定义未来信息在世界动作模型中的角色：既非预测目标，也非正则项，而是可蒸馏的可压缩修正信号。PFD方法在保持纯当前推理接口的同时有效利用了未来信息的指导作用。

Abstract: World action models jointly predict future video and action during training, raising an open question about what role the future-prediction branch actually plays. A recent finding shows that this branch can be removed at inference with little to no loss on common manipulation benchmarks, suggesting that future information may act merely as a regularizer on the shared visual backbone. We propose instead that joint training induces an action-conditioned correction that privileged future observations impose on action denoising, and that current-only policies capture this correction only partially. Making the account precise, we formulate privileged foresight as a residual in the action-denoising direction -- the difference between what a model predicts given the true future and what it predicts given only the current frame -- and introduce \emph{Privileged Foresight Distillation (PFD)}, which transfers this residual from a training-time teacher into a small adapter on a current-only student. The teacher and student share the same backbone and differ only in the attention mask over video tokens; future video is never generated at inference. Controlled experiments verify that this gain reflects a genuine future-conditioned correction rather than a side effect of capacity or regularization. Empirically, PFD achieves consistent improvements on LIBERO and RoboTwin manipulation benchmarks while preserving the current-only inference interface at negligible added latency. This view reframes the role of future information in world action models: not as a target to predict, nor as a regularizer to absorb, but as a compressible correction to be distilled.

</details>


### [137] [Variational Neural Belief Parameterizations for Robust Dexterous Grasping under Multimodal Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25897)
*Clinton Enwerem,Shreya Kalyanaraman,John S. Baras,Calin Belta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于变分推断的抓取规划方法，通过可微高斯混合信念表示和CVaR风险敏感优化，在接触参数和物体位姿不确定下实现鲁棒抓取，相比粒子滤波方法将规划时间降低一个数量级，并保持更低的校准误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统抓取规划使用期望值目标忽略尾部风险，导致在不利接触实现下失败；而风险敏感的POMDP方法依赖粒子滤波信念，存在扩展性差、阻碍梯度优化、CVaR估计方差高等问题。

Method: 将抓取获取建模为隐变量（接触参数、物体位姿）上的变分推断，用可微高斯混合模型表示信念；通过Gumbel-Softmax进行分量选择和重参数化技巧，使样本为信念参数的平滑函数，从而通过可微CVaR替代目标直接优化尾部鲁棒性。

Result: 在仿真中，变分神经信念在接触参数不确定和外力扰动下提高了鲁棒抓取成功率，规划时间减少约一个数量级；在真实机器人上，相比高斯基线方法，控制器以更少步数和更短时间完成抓取，达到更高的触觉抓取质量代理指标；信念校准误差低于0.14（对比CEM方法的0.58）。

Conclusion: 可微高斯混合信念结合CVaR优化是一种高效、准确且鲁棒的抓取规划方案，有效解决了传统粒子滤波方法的扩展性和梯度优化问题，提升了抓取在不确定性下的安全性和效率。

Abstract: Contact variability, sensing uncertainty, and external disturbances make grasp execution stochastic. Expected-quality objectives ignore tail outcomes and often select grasps that fail under adverse contact realizations. Risk-sensitive POMDPs address this failure mode, but many use particle-filter beliefs that scale poorly, obstruct gradient-based optimization, and estimate Conditional Value-at-Risk (CVaR) with high-variance approximations. We instead formulate grasp acquisition as variational inference over latent contact parameters and object pose, representing the belief with a differentiable Gaussian mixture. We use Gumbel-Softmax component selection and location-scale reparameterization to express samples as smooth functions of the belief parameters, enabling pathwise gradients through a differentiable CVaR surrogate for direct optimization of tail robustness. In simulation, our variational neural belief improves robust grasp success under contact-parameter uncertainty and exogenous force perturbations while reducing planning time by roughly an order of magnitude relative to particle-filter model-predictive control. On a serial-chain robot arm with a multifingered hand, we validate grasp-and-lift success under object-pose uncertainty against a Gaussian baseline. Both methods succeed on the tested perturbations, but our controller terminates in fewer steps and less wall-clock time while achieving a higher tactile grasp-quality proxy. Our learned belief also calibrates risk more accurately, keeping mean absolute calibration error below 0.14 across tested simulation regimes, compared with 0.58 for a Cross-Entropy Method planner.

</details>
